## Consequential corrections

- 156-4_065, Opening, terminal overwritten F and low mark: `$\deleted{\unsure{\mathcal F}}\unsure{F_0|F}$` — The possible subscript 0 is retained rather than silently identifying the terminal object with F.
- 156-4_065, Corollaire 1, sauf deletion state: `\deleted{sauf}` — H gives a cancelled exception word as live, changing the displayed condition.
- 156-4_065, Corollaire 1, ending after cancelled pres: `que tout $i$.` — H supplies a finite-exception formulation where the visible live ending is que tout i; the cancelled pres is preserved.
- 156-4_065, Corollaire 3, final map alphabets and separator: `\mathcal F\unsure{\text{---}|\longrightarrow}\prod\mathcal F_i` — The final map connector is not a secure arrow; the uncertainty is preserved instead of silently completing a map.
- 156-4_066, Notations, first live exponent: `\pi_0^{!}` — The live exponent has a distinct upright and a separate lower dot.
- 156-4_066, Notations, second exponent: `\unsure{\underset{\circ}{d}|!}` — A d-shaped closed loop adjoins a thick upright; a separate hollow lower point survives. The first alternative retains both components rather than dropping the lower point. Their correction state remains uncertain.
- 156-4_066, Notations, comparison object after n'est pas: `\pi_0(F,\leq)` — This F has plain top and middle arms, without the looped family form.
- 156-4_066, Notations, third relation in the tuple: `\unsure{\preceq|\leqslant|\deleted{\preceq}|\deleted{\leqslant}}` — There is one heavily worked relation-sized group, with an ascending arm and several bowed returns. I cannot extract a separately legible predecessor or settle whether the entire relation is cancelled.
- 156-4_066, C4 bis, terminal relation: `i\vdash I` — The relation is an upright with one rightward crossbar. It has no rounded left bowl or separate lower arm of the nearby membership sign.
- 156-4_067, First H_i relation to Phi: `H_i\vdash\Phi` — A straight upright with one right-facing middle tick is visible; there is no lower rounded return.
- 156-4_067, Second top set closing mark: `\unsure{\rbrace|)\rbrace}` — The large rounded upper lobe meets the small lower brace-like hook. One worked brace is preferred, but an overlapping parenthesis plus brace cannot be excluded. One group is represented with alternatives, not two certain independent marks.
- 156-4_067, Lower set, tt or universal sign: `\unsure{\forall|\mathrm{tt}}` — Two tall stems meet in an angular inverted-A-like form and share a cross stroke. That shape favours a universal sign, but compressed tt remains possible. The first alternative follows the visible angular connection, not the expected quantifier meaning.
- 156-4_067, First saturée heading, raised family-zero label: `\inserted{\mathcal F_0}` — The zero is a small lower oval and the whole family-zero label is raised between saturée and de. No cancellation line passes through it.
- 156-4_067, Second saturée definition, son support: `\text{cosupport de son support}` — The second term begins with the s-shaped stroke followed immediately by u and two p stems; no co group precedes it. The apparent mathematical mismatch is preserved.
- 156-4_067, Bottom set condition, bar over F: `\overline F\subset M_0` — A distinct horizontal bar stands above F in the set condition. H omits it.
- 156-4_068, Cancelled glyph after Sigma de: `\deleted{$\unsure{\mathscr P|\mathcal F_2|\mathcal F_0|\mathcal F}$}` — The identity of the cancelled mathematical object remains open: a powerset-style capital competes with a family symbol and possible subscript. No certain index or complete extra object is supplied.
- 156-4_068, Revised anti compound: lettering and deletion state: `anti\unsure{\deleted{invo}lution|involution|bijection|dualité}` — The retained correction alternatives replace C's uncertain older isomorph/homéo layers with possible cancellation of invo or the whole-word alternatives involution, bijection and dualité. Its exact property remains unresolved. Old-before-live encoding alone is not a difference in manuscript positions.
- 156-4_069, Parties symbol, high mark at Φ: `$\unsure{\Phi'|\overline\Phi|\Phi}$` — The mark on the family-realization symbol may distinguish a prime from a closure bar. Neither interpretation is substituted solely from the construction being described.
- 156-4_069, Display, last M prime: `\unsure{M'|M''}` — The number of primes on the final quantifier-domain M is left open; it is not forced to match the earlier M-prime merely because that is the expected definition.
- 156-4_070, Opening relation: `\preceq` — Bent upper angle and separately bowed lower stroke, unlike the straight final order sign after avec.
- 156-4_070, Raised relation, trailing dash and state: `G1 raised-node excerpt: \node[anchor=base west] at (0.55,0.58) {\inserted{$F',F''$}};
\node[anchor=base west] at (2.55,0.69) {$\inserted{\unsure{\deleted{\preceq}|\preceq}-}$};` — The subdivision condition’s deletion state remains open. The final transcription does not turn the worked relation into a securely live or securely cancelled assertion.
- 156-4_070, Phrase after figures: `\unsure{toujours|en général|en position}` — Toujours versus en général/en position changes the qualification of the claimed lack of a supremum. The preferred ink reading is retained with alternatives, not promoted to a proved or silently regularized statement.
- 156-4_070, Second subdivision prime count: `$F',F'''$` — The prose names F triple prime as its second subdivision, while the diagram’s lower node is F double prime. The mismatch is retained rather than repaired to the expected pair.
- 156-4_070, Underlined worked adjective: `\uline{comp\unsure{\deleted{ati}ara|\deleted{atib}ara|ata|ati}bles}` — The visible revised term remains uncertain between comparables-like and compatibles/compatables-like lettering. The following explicit rejection of the word compatibles does not by itself settle the overwritten spelling.

- 156-4_070, Diagram upper-right worked sign: `D1 upper-right label: \text{\unsure{\begin{tikzpicture}[baseline=-0.5ex,x=0.32cm,y=0.32cm]

- 156-4_070, Lower note C7 suffix and punctuation: `$C7_0$` — The small closed oval is a lowered zero attached to C7; the marginal instruction refers to C7 subscript 0 rather than unindexed C7.
- 156-4_070, NB overwritten letter before equals: `\deleted{\unsure{F}}G` — The surviving corrected letter is G. Older F-like straight strokes remain uncertain and are retained as an overwritten layer; the final letter is not forced to remain F by the earlier draft.
- 156-4_071, corollary, second order sign: `\preceq` — The second order sign is distinguished from C's alternative sloping inequality; no mathematical claim is checked.
- 156-4_071, inverted application, connecting sign: `\mathrel{\vdash\joinrel\dashv}` — The final glyph preserves the capped horizontal rather than H's mapsto; no bijection assertion is validated.
- 156-4_072, Definition, second order: `\preceq` — Bent upper part and bowed lower stroke differ from the straight preceding ≤.
- 156-4_072, a, individual F in subset condition: `\overline F` — A detached roof lies above that individual F.
- 156-4_072, b, end of cancelled clause: `si $X\in$` — Root a04 covers the deleted clause through the right scan edge: X ∈ is followed by blank space, with no F.
- 156-4_072, Right note b, first strates-like noun: `\unsure{str.tes|strates}` — Initial st and r are visible; a detached medial dot-like mark favours the contraction str.tes. arêtes lacks the visible initial strokes.
- 156-4_072, Right note b, worked initial of second noun: `\unsure{\deleted{S}|S|s}tr\unsure{a|.}tes` — The second noun’s worked initial can be cancelled S, live S or live s; its medial mark can be a or a dot. A secure earlier S layer is not established.
- 156-4_072, Right note b, final G and low tails: `\unsure{G_n|G_m|G}` — Two close low downstrokes follow the G; n is preferred, m or a retraced bare G remain possible.
- 156-4_072, Final body, first G-prime-like capital: `$\unsure{G|\rlap{$\deleted{F}$}G|E}'$` — A looped outside and straight internal bars coexist in one capital position; bare G, an F-under-G overprint, or E remain possible.
- 156-4_072, Lower-left sur note, worked G-like capital: `\unsure{\rlap{$\deleted{F}$}G|G|E}` — An inner F-like stem/top/middle bar lies inside the G-like outer curve at one glyph position.
